route-policy CSR
  if destination in PFX_CSR then
    pass
  elseif community matches-any CS-T1 or community matches-any CS-T2 or community matches-any CS-T3 then
    pass
  elseif community matches-any (8889:8889, 8888:8888) then
    pass
  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy NONE
  drop
end-policy
!
route-policy PASS
  pass
end-policy
!
route-policy LABEL
  if destination in LABEL then
    pass
  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy SID05
  set community (1204:05)
end-policy
!
route-policy U2000
  if destination in U2000 then
    set tag 2
  endif
end-policy

  if destination in cenup then
    drop
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!
route-policy RR-NHS
  if path-type is ebgp then
    set next-hop self
  else
    pass
  endif
end-policy
!

end-policy

  else
    drop
  endif
end-policy

  endif
end-policy
